# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [138] [Near-Optimal Lower Bounds on One-Bit Compressed Sensing of Approximately Sparse Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06750)
*Junren Chen,Arya Mazumdar,Ming Yuan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对近似稀疏信号的一比特压缩感知，给出了首个近最优下界，表明既有误差上界在量级上基本不可改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以缩放的 ℓ1 球或更一般的 ℓq 球刻画近似稀疏性是压缩感知中的常见建模方式，但此前一比特压缩感知主要有上界结果，缺少与之匹配的统一误差下界，因此无法判断现有算法误差率是否本质最优。

Method: 作者在信号集合中嵌入一个小的欧氏球；对均匀抖动模型该步骤较直接，对标准一比特模型则借助 lifting map。随后在该小球内构造两个欧氏距离足够大的信号，使它们在二值观测下不可区分，从而推出统一恢复误差下界。

Result: 在次高斯测量矩阵下，针对标准一比特压缩感知和均匀抖动模型，得到与既有上界近乎匹配的下界；对 ℓq 近似稀疏信号得到下界 Õmega((k/m)^((2-q)/(2+q)))，覆盖 q=0 的精确稀疏与 q=1 的 ℓ1 稀疏情形。

Conclusion: 论文表明近似稀疏一比特压缩感知中的若干已知误差率在对数因子意义下已接近最优，并进一步将理论推广到次 Weibull 矩阵、对抗性比特翻转、矩阵恢复及非稀疏情形的相变刻画。

Abstract: This paper provides the first near-optimal lower bounds for one-bit compressed sensing of approximately sparse signals lying in a scaled $\ell_1$ ball, which is a commonly adopted relaxation of the exactly $k$-sparse assumption. In prior works, the best known upper bounds on uniform Euclidean error are of order $\widetilde{O}((k/m)^{1/3})$, where $m$ is the number of measurements. Under sub-Gaussian matrices, we establish nearly matching lower bounds for both the canonical one-bit compressed sensing model and the uniformly dithered model. Our argument is to first embed a small Euclidean ball into the signal set, which is straightforward for the dithered model but relies on a lifting map for the canonical model, and then construct two signals in this small ball that are separated in Euclidean distance by at least $(k/m)^{1/3}$ (up to logarithmic factor) but are indistinguishable from the binary measurements. Moreover, our argument extends to approximately sparse signals that live in a properly scaled $\ell_q$ ball $(q\in [0,1])$, yielding a lower bound $\widetildeΩ((k/m)^{\frac{2-q}{2+q}})$ that smoothly bridges the cases of exact sparsity ($q=0$) and $\ell_1$ sparsity ($q=1$). Finally, we discuss the extensions of our lower bounds to sub-Weibull matrices, adversarial bit flipping, matrix recovery, and characterize the transition to the non-sparse case.

</details>


### [139] [AirPASS: Over-the-Air Federated Learning via Pinching Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06768)
*Seyed Mohammad Azimi-Abarghouyi,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出 AirPASS，用于多波导 pinching antenna 系统中的 over-the-air federated learning，通过联合优化设备选择、接收波束成形与天线位置，在满足聚合失真约束下尽量选择更多设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AirFL 依赖无线聚合效率，但设备选择、波束成形和 PASS 天线几何位置之间存在强耦合，导致优化问题高度非凸。现有传统共址 MIMO 或调度方法难以同时兼顾学习性能、通信聚合精度和计算复杂度。

Method: 论文构建学习导向的 AirFL 优化问题，以最大化被选设备数量并约束聚合失真。提出交替优化框架 AirPASS：在固定 PASS 配置下，使用 homotopy-Riemannian margin-consolidation 方法联合处理设备选择与接收波束成形；在固定设备集合和波束成形器下，使用 homotopy-assisted geometry optimization 更新 pinching-antenna 位置。

Result: 实验表明，AirPASS 相比传统共址 MIMO 基线性能更优，学习效果接近理想 FedAvg，并且相较 SDR-DC 与 matching-pursuit 调度方案具有更有吸引力的性能—复杂度折中。

Conclusion: 通过将 PASS 的可重构空间几何能力引入 AirFL，并采用同伦辅助的交替优化策略，AirPASS 能有效缓解非凸联合设计难题，在聚合精度、设备参与度和复杂度之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: This paper investigates over-the-air federated learning (AirFL) in wireless systems where the access point is equipped with a multi-waveguide pinching antenna system (PASS). We adopt the widely studied learning-oriented AirFL formulation, which seeks to maximize the number of selected devices while keeping the aggregation distortion below a prescribed threshold. The resulting joint optimization of device selection, receive beamforming, and pinching-antenna placement is highly nonconvex due to the intricate coupling among these system variables. To address this challenge, we develop AirPASS, an alternating optimization framework with two main components: a homotopy-Riemannian margin-consolidation method for device selection and receive beamforming under fixed PASS configuration, and a homotopy-assisted geometry optimization method for updating the pinching-antenna positions under fixed selected devices and beamformer. Experiments show that AirPASS consistently outperforms conventional co-located MIMO baselines, remains close to ideal FedAvg, and achieves an attractive performance-complexity tradeoff relative to SDR-DC and matching-pursuit scheduling alternatives.

</details>


### [140] [Four classes of few-weight self-orthogonal codes and their applications for LCD codes and quantum codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07181)
*Yue Huang,Zhonghao Liang,Chenlu Jia,Yongkang Wan,Qunying Liao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文基于两类定义集研究增广码，构造若干四重量自正交线性码，并进一步得到相关LCD码和量子码，其中部分码达到或接近经典界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自正交码、少重量码、LCD码和量子码在编码理论与密码学中具有重要应用；已有工作通过增广码构造少重量自正交码，本文旨在扩展该思路并获得性能更优或具有明确参数的新码族。

Method: 针对两类定义集，分析其对应增广码的结构与重量分布，构造一类投影四重量自正交码和三类四重量自正交码；对其中两类码进一步确定其对偶码参数，并利用这些结果构造LCD码和量子码。

Result: 得到多类四重量自正交线性码；确定两类码的对偶码参数；构造两类LCD码和一类量子码。部分LCD码的对偶码相对于球包装界几乎最优，所构造的一类量子码相对于量子Singleton界为AMDS。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了基于定义集与增广码的少重量自正交码构造方法，并展示了所得码在LCD码和量子码构造中的应用价值，尤其在接近最优或AMDS性能方面具有理论意义。

Abstract: Since self-orthogonal codes, few-weight codes, linear complementary dual codes(LCD codes, for short) and quantum codes have nice applications in coding theory and cryptography, they have received continuous attention. In 2024, by introducing the notion of the augment code, Heng et al.[30] constructed several classes of few-weight self-orthogonal codes basing on defining sets, which are introduced by Ding et al.[10] in 2007. In this manuscript, for two classes of defining sets, we consider the corresponding augmented codes, construct a class of projective four-weight self-orthogonal codes and three classes of four-weight self-orthogonal codes. And for two classes of these four-weight self-orthogonal linear codes, we determine the parameters of their dual codes. As applications, we construct two classes of LCD codes and a class of quantum codes. In particular, we prove that there exists a class of these LCD codes whose dual codes are almost optimal LCD codes according to the sphere packing bound, and a class of quantum codes are AMDS according to the quantum Singleton bound.

</details>
